what are the top 10 side effects reported for drug tromethamine?
PRED: select t1.side_effect from ( select side_effects.side_effect, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from side_effects join prescriptions on side_effects.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tromethamine' group by side_effects.side_effect ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 10
GOLD: null

what are the top 10 side effects reported for drug tromethamine?
----------
PREDS:
select t1.side_effect from ( select side_effects.side_effect, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from side_effects join prescriptions on side_effects.hadm_id = prescriptions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'tromethamine' group by side_effects.side_effect ) as t1 where t1.c1 <= 10
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------


what are the 10 most recent papers on long-term use of insulin?
PRED: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long-term (current) use of insulin' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 10 )
GOLD: null

what are the 10 most recent papers on long-term use of insulin?
----------
PREDS:
select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t3.icd9_code from ( select t2.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long-term (current) use of insulin' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t2.charttime) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 month') group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 10 )
----------
TRUTH:
null

PRED: select t2.drug from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures.charttime from procedures join admissions on procedures.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'total splenectomy' ) ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and datetime(t2.startdate) between datetime(t1.charttime) and datetime(t1.charttime,'+2 day')
GOLD: null

----------
PREDS:

select t3.SHORT_TITLE from ( select t2.SHORT_TITLE, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select diagnoses.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join diagnoses on prescriptions.hadm_id = diagnoses.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'propranolol hcl' ) as t1 join ( select diagnoses.subject_id, diagnoses.SHORT_TITLE, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join diagnoses on prescriptions.hadm_id = diagnoses.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.startdate < t2.startdate group by t2.SHORT_TITLE ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 10
----------
TRUTH:
null
----------

what's up with the top 10 most recent papers on long-term use of insulin?
PRED: select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'insulin' and datetime(prescriptions.startdate) >= datetime(current_time,'-10 year') ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where datetime(prescriptions.startdate) >= datetime(current_time,'-10 year') ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.startdate < t2.startdate and datetime(t2.startdate) between datetime(t1.startdate) and datetime(t1.startdate,'+2 month') group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 10
GOLD: null

list the drugs that should not be administered during procedure-one vessel?
PRED: select t3.drug from ( select t2.drug, dense_rank() over ( order by count(*) desc ) as c1 from ( select admissions.subject_id, procedures.charttime, admissions.hadm_id from procedures join admissions on procedures.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures.short_title = 'procedure-one vessel' ) as t1 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, admissions.hadm_id from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.startdate and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id group by t2.drug ) as t3 where t3.c1 <= 5
GOLD: null

list the drugs that should not be administered during procedure-one vessel?

what's the price of a excis lesion of muscle?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'procedures_icd' and cost.event_id in ( select procedures_icd.row_id from procedures_icd where procedures_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'excision of lesion of muscle' ) )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'excision of lesion of muscle ) )']]}
----------
TRUTH:

{'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost'], 'tab': ['cost'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'excis lesion of muscle ) )']]}

calculate the duration of stay of patient 85169 of the first hospital stay.
----------
PREDS:
select strftime('%j',DEMOGRAPHIC.DISCHTIME) - strftime('%j',DEMOGRAPHIC.ADMITTIME) from DEMOGRAPHIC where DEMOGRAPHIC.SUBJECT_ID = 85169 and DEMOGRAPHIC.DISCHTIME is not null order by DEMOGRAPHIC.ADMITTIME asc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['demographic.dischtime', 'is', 'not null order by demographic.admittime asc limit 1'], ['demographic.subject_id', '=', '85169']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select strftime('%j',admissions.dischtime) - strftime('%j',admissions.admittime) from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 85169 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.dischtime', 'admissions.admittime'], 'tab': ['admissions'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '85169']]}

how much difference is patient 7107's glucose level last measured on the first hospital visit compared to the second to last value measured on the first hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 7107 ) and icustays.outtime is not null order by icustays.intime asc limit 1 ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'glucose' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 ) - ( select chartevents.valuenum from chartevents where chartevents.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 7107 ) and icustays.outtime is not null order by icustays.intime asc limit 1 ) and chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'glucose' and d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['chartevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['chartevents'], 'cond': [['d_items.label', '=', 'glucose'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents ) order by chartevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 7107 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'glucose' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 ) - ( select labevents.valuenum from labevents where labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 7107 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) and labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'glucose' ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'glucose ) order by labevents.charttime desc limit 1 offset 1 )']]}
----------

how was patient 26156 first admitted to the hospital until 2104?
----------
PREDS:

{'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.admission_type'], 'tab': ['admissions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '26156'], ['datetime(admissions.admittime)', '<=', 'datetime(current_time, -2104 year ) order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1']]}

since 4 years ago is any organism in the first urine test of patient 59941?
----------

what is the one year survival rate of the depress psychosis-unspec patients?
----------
PREDS:
select sum( case when demographic.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',demographic.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 1 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select demographic.subject_id, diagnoses.charttime from diagnoses join demographic on diagnoses.hadm_id = demographic.hadm_id where diagnoses.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'depress psychosis-unspec' ) group by demographic.subject_id having min(diagnoses.charttime) = diagnoses.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 1 * 365 ) as t2 join demographic on t2.subject_id = demographic.subject_id
{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [["strftime('%j',current_time)", '-', 'strftime( %j ,t1.charttime) > 1 * 365 ) as t2 join demographic on t2.subject_id = demographic.subject_id']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t2.charttime) > 1 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'depress psychosis-unspec' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 1 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['patients.dod', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': [], 'cond': [["strftime('%j',current_time)", '-', 'strftime( %j ,t1.charttime) > 1 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id']]}

how many patients were diagnosed with esophageal reflux after they were diagnosed with single lb in-hosp w cs within the same hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select count( distinct t1.subject_id ) from ( select diagnoses.subject_id, diagnoses.charttime, diagnoses.hadm_id from diagnoses join admissions on diagnoses.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses.short_title = 'esophageal reflux' ) as t1 join ( select diagnoses.subject_id, diagnoses.charttime, diagnoses.hadm_id from diagnoses join admissions on diagnoses.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses.short_title = 'single lb in-hosp w cs' ) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime > t2.charttime and t1.hadm_id = t2.hadm_id
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['t1.charttime', '>', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}
----------
TRUTH:
